- Move to g3: Attacked by Black Queen at d3 (diagonal d3-e3-f3-g3)? No, d3-e3-f3-g3 is not a diagonal. Diagonal d3-e2-f1 or d3-c2-b1 or d3-c4-b5... Wait, d3 to g3 is a rank. d3-e3-f3-g3. Yes, the Black Queen at d3 attacks g3.
- Move to f3: Attacked by Black Queen at d3 (rank d3-e3-f3).
- Move to f2: Attacked by Black Queen at d2 (rank d2-e2-f2).
- Move to f1: Attacked by Black Queen at d3 (diagonal d3-e2-f1).

So, the legal moves are g2h1 and g2g1.
Between moving to h1 and g1, h1 is further from the action and keeps the king safer in the corner, but g1 is also an option.
Usually, in this kind of endgame, trying to avoid checks is key.
The position seems weird (two black queens?), but I must follow the legal moves.

Let's pick `g2h1`.
